## 1. Purpose and authority

This is the canonical mutable ledger for **longitudinal character state** in the Project SEKAI analytical layer. It stores state transitions that later event, card, area, self, special, and My SEKAI analysis can extend without overwriting historically valid earlier states.

The ledger is not a character monograph. It should remain compact enough to answer:

> **At temporal boundary T, what psychological/behavioral state is this character in, what changed to produce that state, and which later states supersede it only as the default current state rather than as historical truth?**

Current populated N25 entries integrate the completed Nightcord at 25:00 main-story foundation plus positive character-state evidence through EVENT_0053. EVENT_0030, EVENT_0031, EVENT_0037, and EVENT_0040 were fully screened at I0; EVENT_0038 supplied contextual Rui-side evidence without a human state delta. EVENT_0039 advances Mizuki to `MZ-E0039-01`; EVENT_0041 adds post-crisis ordinary-life evidence without a successor state; EVENT_0042 advances Mafuyu to `MF-E0042-01` while preserving Kanade's `K-E0026-01` with major ordinary-life/self-recognition refinement; EVENT_0051 adds dense Mizuki ordinary-social/recipient-care evidence plus bounded Kanade preference and Mafuyu accommodation rules without a successor state; EVENT_0052 adds a bounded Mizuki garment-material/craft-support competence domain without a successor state. EVENT_0053 advances Ena to `E-E0053-01`, stress-testing the EVENT_0014 continuation rule under sustained technical inferiority, peer comparison, harsh criticism, and uncertain future talent. N25 entries inherit from:

- `PJSK_N25_MAIN_STORY_PHASE_MAP.md`
- `PJSK_N25_MS_P01_DEEP_READING.md` through `PJSK_N25_MS_P05_DEEP_READING.md`
- `PJSK_N25_MAIN_STORY_SYNTHESIS.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0002_DEEP_READING.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0004_N25_INTEGRATION_CHECKPOINT.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0007_DEEP_READING.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0009_N25_INTEGRATION_CHECKPOINT.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0014_DEEP_READING.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0019_DEEP_READING.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0026_DEEP_READING.md`
- `PJSK_EVENT_0029_N25_INTEGRATION_CHECKPOINT.md`
- EVENT_0032 bounded area evidence recorded directly in this ledger / relationship / epistemic ledgers
- `PJSK_EVENT_0033_N25_INTEGRATION_CHECKPOINT.md`
- EVENT_0034 bounded card evidence (`PJSK:card:0281:01-02`) recorded directly in the character / relationship / epistemic / claim / theme ledgers
- `PJSK_EVENT_0035_DEEP_READING.md`

## 2. State model

A state record should normally contain:

- **state_id** — stable analytical identifier;
- **character**;
- **valid_from / valid_through** — source or analytical boundary;
- **status** — historical / current-within-cutoff / superseded-as-default;
- **self-concept**;
- **goals**;
- **active fears / vulnerabilities**;
- **emotional baseline**;
- **agency profile**;
- **coping / defenses**;
- **relationship conditions**;
- **knowledge constraints** — pointer to epistemic ledger rather than duplication when possible;
- **speech/register changes**;
- **transition trigger**;
- **key evidence**;
- **confidence**;
- **next-state pointer**.

Historical states are append-only. A later state may become the default reconstruction state without invalidating an earlier state for historically bounded scenarios.

## 3. N25 state initialization

### 3.1 Asahina Mafuyu

#### MF-M0 — pre-rupture Snow / ordinary-function state

**Boundary:** P01 through early P02.  
**Status:** historical.  
**Core state:** Public competence and Snow collaboration are stable while severe self-discontinuity remains mostly concealed.

- **Self-concept:** weakly accessible; externally successful roles remain easier to inhabit than first-person preference.
- **Goals:** continue Nightcord work; continue OWN search; satisfy ordinary school/family expectations.
- **Preference access:** impaired but not absent.
- **Emotional baseline:** flattened/uncertain privately, socially fluent publicly.
- **Agency:** high in role-compliant execution; low-to-variable in self-authored life choice.
- **Coping:** compliance, anticipatory role performance, compartmentalization across public/Snow/private/OWN contexts.
- **Relationship condition:** Nightcord is meaningful but anonymous; others lack offline identity/context.
- **Knowledge constraint:** group does not know Snow = OWN or the developmental basis of Mafuyu's condition.
- **Speech/register:** polished public/Snow language; markedly flatter private language.
- **Transition trigger:** exposure of Snow/OWN discontinuity, disappearance, Empty SEKAI confrontation.
- **Key evidence:** P01; early P02.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** MF-M1.

#### MF-M1 — exposed Empty-SEKAI rupture state

**Boundary:** late P02 through P03.  
**Status:** historical.

- **Self-concept:** distrusts socially successful roles as adequate representations of self.
- **Goals:** isolation; continue/complete self-search if possible; escape externally defined roles.
- **Active fears:** no true self exists; Nightcord cannot help; continued social role performance is meaningless.
- **Emotional baseline:** blunt rejection, irritability, alienation, high friction.
- **Agency:** strong in withdrawal and boundary rejection; weak in constructive self-definition.
- **Coping:** isolation, repudiation of Snow as sufficient identity, diagnostic attacks on others' hidden wounds.
- **Relationship condition:** Nightcord has crossed into crisis knowledge but still lacks full causes.
- **Speech/register:** terse, direct, minimally mitigated Empty-SEKAI crisis register.
- **Transition trigger:** continued failure of self-search; growing exhaustion of hope; later intervention.
- **Key evidence:** P02-P03.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** MF-M2.

#### MF-M2 — terminal search-exhaustion state

**Boundary:** late P04 through P05 terminal crisis.  
**Status:** historical high-stakes state.

- **Self-concept:** self-search interpreted as failed; disappearance temporarily experienced as the clearest authentic answer.
- **Goals:** end the search/hope/failure cycle; avoid further externally imposed answers.
- **Active fears:** renewed hope followed by renewed failure; intervention becoming another demand.
- **Emotional baseline:** exhausted, rejecting, yet cognitively incisive about others' motives.
- **Agency:** high in refusing intervention and naming selfish/coercive dynamics.
- **Coping:** withdrawal, certainty-seeking through disappearance, rejection of praise/promises.
- **Relationship condition:** others' attachment is experienced as intrusive until they present first-person relational reasons rather than authoritative definitions of Mafuyu.
- **Speech/register:** severe crisis directness; high capacity to identify hypocrisy, ego, and unwanted obligation.
- **Transition trigger:** Kanade/Ena/Mizuki interventions plus recognition that disappearance is genuine but not exhaustive; recovery of desire to be found.
- **Key evidence:** P04-P05.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** MF-M3.

#### MF-M3 — post-main provisional-continuation state

**Boundary:** end of `nightcode_01_20`.  
**Endpoint alias:** `N25-MF-01`.  
**Status:** historical foundation endpoint after EVENT_0002 integration.

- **Self-concept:** incomplete; recognizes wanting to be found but lacks stable comprehensive self-definition.
- **Primary goal:** continue searching for real feeling/self while remaining with Nightcord for now.
- **Secondary goals:** continue creating; understand the recurring “Mafuyu sound”; maintain newly meaningful relationships.
- **Active fears:** hope followed by failure; never finding herself; externally authored future; return to roles defined only by others.
- **Preference access:** weak in many mundane domains, not absent.
- **Emotional access:** variable; flattening remains, sharper affect under stress, emerging relational warmth.
- **Agency:** low-to-variable in externally structured choice; significant in withdrawal, confrontation, continued search, chosen proximity, and private contact.
- **Coping:** increasing candor about uncertainty; creative search; selective relational mirroring.
- **Relationship condition:** Kanade central but not exclusive; Mizuki structurally empathic; Ena high-friction attachment; Nightcord increasingly safe for uncertainty.
- **Knowledge constraint:** does not possess the audience's full correction to Kanade's guilt narrative.
- **Speech/register:** post-main private/Nightcord register permits “I don't know” instead of automatically producing a socially correct answer.
- **Key evidence:** P05 and synthesis §17.1 / §25.1.
- **Confidence:** high for main-story endpoint.
- **Next:** `MF-E0002-01`.

### 3.2 Yoisaki Kanade

#### K-M0 — stable rescue-mission baseline

**Boundary:** P01 through early P02.  
**Status:** historical.

- **Self-concept:** composer whose moral purpose is to create music capable of saving others.
- **Goals:** compose continuously; make people happy/save through music.
- **Vulnerabilities:** guilt, responsibility inflation, poor self-care.
- **Agency:** high under task conditions; quietness does not imply passivity.
- **Coping:** work, technical control, mission persistence.
- **Relationship condition:** Snow's “saved me” testimony functions as validating evidence for Kanade's entire post-trauma project.
- **Transition trigger:** Mafuyu reframes that “saving” as insufficient for finding herself.
- **Key evidence:** P01-P02.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** K-M1.

#### K-M1 — failed-rescue overfunctioning

**Boundary:** P03.  
**Status:** historical.

- **Self-concept:** rescue mission threatened by apparent failure.
- **Goals:** compensate by doing more, including absorbing Snow's work.
- **Vulnerabilities:** workload expansion, food/sleep deterioration, intolerance of interruption.
- **Agency:** extremely high but maladaptively narrowed.
- **Coping:** overfunctioning before collapse.
- **Speech/register:** increasingly terse; concern can be treated as obstruction.
- **Transition trigger:** exhaustion and loss of confidence in whether continued composition has meaning.
- **Key evidence:** P03.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** K-M2.

#### K-M2 — collapse / withdrawal

**Boundary:** P03-P04 transition.  
**Status:** historical high-stakes state.

- **Self-concept:** mission temporarily loses justificatory power.
- **Goals:** withdraw into low-demand existence rather than outwardly dramatize despair.
- **Vulnerabilities:** explicit disappearance/withdrawal desire; inability to accept multicausal correction to guilt.
- **Coping:** retreat; reduction of demands.
- **Transition trigger:** reconstruction of father history, Miku's “reaching” category, recognition that partial efficacy may exist.
- **Key evidence:** P04.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** K-M3.

#### K-M3 — reflexive recommitment

**Boundary:** P04 through early P05.  
**Status:** historical/intervention state.

- **Self-concept:** still rescue-oriented, but increasingly aware that intervention contains ego and can cause harm.
- **Goals:** reach/save Mafuyu despite uncertainty.
- **Moral cognition:** sophisticated self-critique without release from obligation.
- **Agency:** high; acts after explicit uncertainty rather than because uncertainty disappears.
- **Coping:** composition, self-disclosure, continued action.
- **Transition trigger:** reciprocal promise with Mafuyu.
- **Key evidence:** P04-P05.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** K-M4.

#### K-M4 — reciprocal-curse endpoint

**Boundary:** end of `nightcode_01_20`.  
**Endpoint alias:** `N25-KF-01`.  
**Status:** historical foundation endpoint after EVENT_0002 integration.

- **Self-concept:** composer who believes she must continue, now aware rescue is partly guilt/ego.
- **Primary goals:** create music that can save/reach; keep Mafuyu's search going.
- **Active fears:** causing harm; failing another person; witnessing disappearance; losing the moral structure that justifies continued creation.
- **Agency:** high under task/rescue conditions.
- **Self-care:** poor baseline; acute deterioration under mission pressure.
- **Moral cognition:** prone to responsibility inflation but capable of substantial self-critique.
- **Relationship condition:** Mafuyu's continuation is linked to Kanade's ongoing creation; Kanade's continuation is partly linked to Mafuyu's unfinished search. Relationship is life-sustaining and dependency-risking at once.
- **Knowledge constraint:** does not know Mafuyu's complete childhood identity-erosion sequence.
- **Reconstruction default:** persistent, quiet, self-aware but **not boundary-cured**.
- **Key evidence:** P05 and synthesis §17.2 / §25.2.
- **Confidence:** high for main-story endpoint.
- **Next:** `K-E0002-01`.

### 3.3 Shinonome Ena

#### E-M0 — recognition-sensitive collaborator

**Boundary:** P01.  
**Status:** historical.

- **Self-concept:** artist; artistic identity highly valued.
- **Goals:** improve and receive meaningful artistic recognition.
- **Vulnerabilities:** talent insecurity; distinction between appearance attention and art recognition.
- **Relationship condition:** Snow's specific praise matters.
- **Next:** E-M1.

**Boundary:** P02-P03.

- **Self-concept:** artistic legitimacy acutely threatened by talent asymmetry.
- **Goals:** prove worth; protect dignity.
- **Vulnerabilities:** pity, mockery, condescension, authoritative invalidation.
- **Coping:** shame → anger → effort/competition → despair → substitute validation.
- **Relationship condition:** Snow praise is retroactively suspected of pity/mockery after OWN reveal.
- **Disappearance vulnerability:** explicit.
- **Next:** E-M2.

#### E-M2 — refusal / boundary state

**Boundary:** P04.  
**Status:** historical.

- **Core state:** resentment remains high; Ena rejects the premise that Mafuyu's suffering creates an unlimited rescue obligation.
- **Care:** persists at the group boundary; she wants Kanade/Mizuki to return safely.
- **Agency:** high and boundary-preserving.
- **Next:** E-M3.

#### E-M3 — antagonistic-attachment endpoint

**Boundary:** end of `nightcode_01_20`.  
**Endpoint alias:** `N25-EF-01`.  
**Status:** **historical after EVENT_0014; superseded as current by `E-E0014-01`.**

- **Self-concept:** artist whose desired identity remains contingent on talent and meaningful recognition.
- **Primary goal:** improve and become recognized as the artist she wants to be.
- **Active fears:** mediocrity, pity, condescension, authoritative invalidation, invisibility of valued creative self.
- **Agency:** high, often verbal and confrontational.
- **Coping:** anger, effort, competition, selective social validation.
- **Relationship condition:** Mafuyu = unresolved antagonistic attachment; Mizuki = easy banter plus increased serious understanding; Kanade = creative trust and practical concern.
- **Knowledge constraint:** do not assume full Kanade-father disclosure unless later source confirms transmission.
- **Reconstruction default:** direct, easily activated by artistic hierarchy, caring without necessarily becoming gentle.
- **Confidence:** high as the pre-EVENT_0014 state; EVENT_0014 supplies the developmental transition.
- **Next:** `E-E0014-01`.

- **Goals:** create, socialize, preserve enjoyment and self-expression.

- **Preference/affect access:** severe ordinary preference difficulty persists. EVENT_0035 adds a stronger positive-affect pathway: warmth can be felt, associated with a specific childhood care memory, and preserved even while Mafuyu still cannot categorically name the feeling or derive a broad preference map from it.
- **Autobiographical access:** childhood memories can now function as affective evidence, not merely factual history. The rabbit-shaped apple, being cared for while ill, and hand-holding become a retrievable positive-affect anchor. Memory remains painful as well as warm.
- **Agency:** materially increased in the self-search domain. Mafuyu independently asks why she smiled, retrieves and examines the album, asks Kanade about the feeling behind her song, returns to the question after illness, asks Miku to sing draft lyrics for discrimination, revises wording, and decides `自分のために` to preserve the feeling `私の言葉で`.
- **Coping/search method:** `embodied affect -> associative memory -> relational/creative cue -> self-authored lyric -> preserved trace -> further discrimination`. This extends rather than discards EVENT_0002's creative externalization method.
- **Interoception/self-care:** still impaired. Mafuyu overcommits across school, prep study, kyudo, committee work, and N25 production, misses lunch, fails to recognize illness adequately, and collapses. Affective progress does not imply healthy body-state monitoring.
- **Family condition:** positive childhood attachment/care memory and current autonomy-eroding expectation coexist. The source does not authorize either a wholly malicious-mother model or a reconciliation/healthy-family model.
- **Relationship condition:** Kanade remains a powerful affective/creative catalyst and provides embodied care, but Kanade does not author Mafuyu's recovered meaning. N25 supports the search; Miku can act as a non-forcing co-memory/feedback partner.
- **Speech/register:** `わからない` remains legitimate and frequent, but it can now coexist with first-person preservation language: Mafuyu can say she does not know what the feeling is while also saying she wants to keep it, write it, and do so in her own words.
- **Transition trigger:** EVENT_0026 warmth/smile residue -> EVENT_0035 self-initiated inquiry -> childhood album/illness memory -> Kanade's family-memory explanation of the song -> lyric externalization -> recognition that the feeling can be experienced and preserved without full naming.
- **Guardrail:** do not reconstruct this as broad recovery of taste, ordinary preference, stable self-definition, maternal reconciliation, cured disappearance risk, or healthy self-care. The transition is from *affect can be mine* to *positive affect can be autobiographically recovered and deliberately preserved as mine*.
- **Key evidence:** `PJSK:event:0035:01-08`; cards `PJSK:card:0284:01-02`, especially `0284:02`; supporting cards `0285-0288`; linked area `areatalk_ev_night_05_001-007`.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** `MF-E0042-01`.

Kanade also demonstrates emergency caregiving and reciprocal domestic intimacy. Persistent guilt, rescue responsibility, and poor self-care remain active.

## 3.18 EVENT_0039 state integration - Mizuki attachment-preserving disclosure deferral transition

### MZ-E0039-01 - attachment-preserving disclosure deferral

**Boundary:** end of EVENT_0039 `ボクのあしあと キミのゆくさき`.  
**Previous state:** `MZ-E0019-01`.  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.  
**Transition class:** developmental / attachment-mediated disclosure deferral.

- **Self-concept:** preserve the known-self model. Mizuki continues to know preferred aesthetics, social presentation, and the legitimacy of being `ボクは、ボク`; the conflict is not uncertainty about what kind of person Mizuki is but whether a guarded self-bound fact can enter an increasingly valuable relationship without reorganizing it.
- **Primary goals:** remain with N25; preserve ordinary unselfconscious friendship for as long as possible; retain disclosure ownership; continue creating, joking, shopping, and participating normally; if possible, someday reconcile being known with remaining relationally safe.
- **Active fears:** involuntary/indirect exposure as social networks overlap; post-disclosure relational reclassification; kindness that becomes self-conscious accommodation; losing the ordinary texture of the current friendship; hurting Ena through continued withholding; being unable to reciprocate the explicit friendship Ena offers.
- **Emotional baseline:** ordinary pleasure, fashion interest, humor, social initiative, and N25 attachment remain genuine. Acute disclosure cues can produce racing arousal, overnight rumination, avoidance, pallor/exhaustion, sudden flight, and catastrophic escape thoughts without eliminating ordinary positive affect.
- **Agency:** high across social planning, creative work, fashion, care, and school interaction; sharply impaired around guarded-content disclosure. EVENT_0039 adds deliberate temporal agency inside avoidance: Mizuki recognizes that not telling may prolong the current relationship.
- **Coping/defenses:** bright-register intensification, humor, redirection, plausible illness excuses, Nightcord avoidance, thought suppression (`今までどおり`), compartmentalization, abrupt flight under pressure, Empty-SEKAI refuge, and rooftop/Rui safe-return remain active. The new element is **conscious disclosure deferral as relationship preservation**.
- **Relationship condition:** N25 is now explicitly one of the places where Mizuki feels most able to be themself and therefore carries extreme loss stakes. Ena has explicitly named the friendship and promised non-extractive waiting; Rui remains a companion who `孤独を知る`; MEIKO offers low-demand refuge; Mafuyu functions as a progress comparison/foil without knowing the guarded issue.
- **Knowledge constraint:** Mizuki knows Ena will wait and Rui recognizes the waiting structure; N25 still does not know the guarded content. EVENT_0038 Rui childhood detail remains audience-richer except where Rui directly speaks about himself in card `0307:02`.
- **Speech/register:** distress can coexist with highly fluent joking. Under direct disclosure pressure, tone may shift from playful deflection to unusually hard refusal (`話せることなんてない`) and flight. After autonomy is restored, Mizuki can return quickly to affectionate ordinary banter.
- **Reconstruction default:** greater trust does not monotonically increase disclosure probability. At this state, greater attachment increases both the wish to answer trust honestly and the incentive to defer disclosure to protect the relationship's present form.
- **Guardrail:** do not model Mizuki as having decided never to disclose, as secretly distrusting N25's kindness, as identity-confused, or as globally withdrawn. The endpoint lie concerns the current future-disclosure promise and preservation motive, not the reality of affection or enjoyment.
- **Key evidence:** `PJSK:event:0039:01-08`, especially `0039:03`, `0039:05`, `0039:07-08`; `PJSK:card:0307:01-02`; `PJSK:card:0308:02`; `PJSK:card:0311:02`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_night_06_005:01`.
- **Confidence:** high for the state transition; guarded-content specifics remain intentionally unresolved.
- **Next:** `PENDING_NEXT_RELEVANT_RELEASE`.

### Preserved global states - Mafuyu, Kanade, Ena

- **Mafuyu:** preserve `MF-E0035-01`. Card `0311` materially strengthens self-authored affect preservation into creative authorship: Mafuyu accepts herself as a legitimate addressee for lyrics and tentatively agrees to write more from felt/thought material. Her later answer to Mizuki about painful progress is direct self-knowledge, not a new global state.
- **Kanade:** preserve `K-E0026-01`. Card `0309` strengthens intermediate efficacy and positive-destination composition through broader listener response and a cautious father-facing application, without resolving guilt or self-neglect.
- **Ena:** preserve `E-E0014-01`. EVENT_0039 changes Ena's relationship-specific support rule toward Mizuki—away from disclosure demand and toward explicit waiting—but does not replace her governing artistic self state.

**Authority result:** EVENT_0039 advances Mizuki `MZ-E0019-01 -> MZ-E0039-01`. Current tuple becomes `MF-E0035-01 / K-E0026-01 / E-E0014-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.

## 3.20 EVENT_0042 state integration - relational warmth / contribution-linked self-evidence transition

### MF-E0042-01 - relational warmth / contribution-linked self-evidence

**Boundary:** end of complete EVENT_0042 `交わる旋律 灯るぬくもり` review envelope.  
**Previous state:** `MF-E0035-01`.  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.  
**Transition class:** developmental / relational positive affect, contribution-linked self-evidence, and emergent communicative authorship.

- **Self-concept:** still incomplete, but Mafuyu now has direct first-person evidence that positive affect can arise from another person's happiness and from recognizing that her own small action helped produce that happiness. Relational consequence becomes self-evidence before a categorical relationship label is available.
- **Primary goals:** continue the self/feeling search; investigate unexplained positive affect rather than dismiss it; preserve or revisit meaningful relational traces; continue lyric work from material that may be her own even before she can fully explain it.
- **Preference/affect access:** broad ordinary preference access remains severely impaired. EVENT_0042 adds a new trigger class: Kanade's visible happiness, Mafuyu's contribution to it, and later promise/follow-through toward Miku can generate positive affect without an immediate music or autobiographical-memory trigger.
- **Relational affect:** Mafuyu twice notices chest warmth around Kanade's happiness and eventually identifies the concrete causal relation `my small action helped Kanade -> Kanade is happy -> my chest feels warm`. She can evaluate the sensation as `悪い感覚ではない` without calling it friendship, love, altruism, or preference.
- **Agency:** increases again in the self-search domain. Mafuyu chooses to attend an otherwise unnecessary mixed-unit meeting specifically because it may help explain the warmth; independently asks what Miku means to her; requests the N25 song archive in creation order; later makes, remembers, and keeps a promise to show Miku snow.
- **Creative authorship:** EVENT_0035 self-authored preservation persists and gains tentative outward orientation. Mafuyu recognizes that when Kanade's song made her smile she wanted to leave her own feeling in the lyrics, that being heard by someone is preferable to no one hearing them, and asks whether she may actually be putting something into the words. This is emergent communicative authorship, not a settled artistic mission.
- **Coping/search method:** `unexpected relational affect -> preserve uncertainty -> voluntarily enter relevant relational context -> compare others' legible desires/communication -> test through creative/relational history -> act on low-stakes promise -> retain first-person evidence without premature label`.
- **Virtual Singer relation:** N25 Miku becomes an explicit object of Mafuyu's self-inquiry rather than only a mirror/tool. Mafuyu can also act for Miku's ordinary desire and experience satisfaction in keeping the promise.
- **Interoception/self-care:** still impaired. Linked area `_002` shows school obligations consuming lunch and Mafuyu privately deciding she can simply skip eating until Ichika intervenes. Relational development does not imply healthy body-state monitoring or workload boundaries.
- **Public/private mode:** public competence remains extremely high. Mafuyu can mentor Ichika, manage committee work, and provide technically sophisticated lyric advice while private affective interpretation remains incomplete.
- **Family condition:** unchanged from `MF-E0035-01`; EVENT_0042 does not resolve maternal/autonomy conflict.
- **Relationship condition:** Kanade becomes not only a catalyst/caregiver but a person whose ordinary happiness can itself positively affect Mafuyu. N25 Miku becomes a reciprocal self-search companion. Ichika becomes a bounded school/creative catalyst without receiving private N25 biography.
- **Speech/register:** `わからない` remains legitimate; the new endpoint adds cautious positive appraisal (`悪い感覚ではない`) and behavior that can precede explicit emotional naming.
- **Transition trigger:** Kanade/Ichika shared-CD encounter -> Kanade happiness -> first relational warmth -> Miku prompts self-chosen investigation -> mixed-unit creative exchange -> recognition of emergent outward lyric intention -> Kanade thanks Mafuyu -> repeated contribution-linked warmth -> card-level relational inquiry and promise follow-through.
- **Guardrail:** do not reconstruct this as broad preference recovery, generalized love of helping people, stable altruistic identity, fluent emotion naming, family resolution, healthy self-care, or disappearance-risk cure. The developmental change is the addition of **relational consequence as usable first-person evidence**.
- **Key evidence:** `PJSK:event:0042:01-08`, especially `0042:04-05` and `0042:07-08`; `PJSK:card:0324:01-02`; `PJSK:card:0326:01-02`; linked `areatalk_ev_shuffle_14_001-002` and `_008`.
- **Confidence:** high.
- **Next:** `PENDING_NEXT_RELEVANT_RELEASE`.

### Kanade - preserve `K-E0026-01` with major ordinary-life/self-recognition refinement

- Kanade's rescue/penance architecture, father-guilt belief, responsibility inflation, and poor self-care remain active; no successor global state is warranted.
- EVENT_0042 nevertheless proves Kanade can pursue music from personal curiosity, feel glad that another fan shares a creator she likes, enjoy a mixed-unit meeting without rescue utility, and explicitly want future contact because the conversation was fun.
- Card `0327:02` converts this from scene-local pleasure into self-recognition: Kanade remembers ordinary childhood friendship enjoyment she had forgotten and wonders whether, like Mafuyu, she has forgotten/lost parts of life. She judges noticing that possibility as potentially good.
- Reconstruction should therefore permit non-instrumental social pleasure and awareness of mission-narrowing while retaining the high-stakes pull of `K-E0026-01`.

## 3.22 EVENT_0047 state integration - retrospective causal reconstruction without successor global state

**Event:** `EVENT_0047`.
**Impact:** Tier B relationship/origin revelation; secondary Tier C historical characterization and Tier D ordinary-life/reconstruction evidence; I2 interpretive refinement.
**State result:** preserve `MF-E0042-01 / K-E0026-01 / E-E0014-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.

### Kanade - preserve `K-E0026-01`; add pre-N25 causal reconstruction

EVENT_0047 directly shows the post-father-collapse rescue command before Snow becomes Kanade's collaborator. Listener relief briefly validates Kanade, then immediately expands into a larger obligation: more people must be saved, and successful rescue is tied to whether Kanade has a right to remain. Food, sleep, grandmother contact, and escalating physical symptoms are subordinated to composition until Kanade loses consciousness.

This is historical reconstruction rather than a new current state. It strongly raises confidence that `K-E0026-01`'s present rescue/penance architecture has a long pre-N25 causal lineage and that severe self-neglect is mission-linked rather than a harmless dietary quirk.

Present EVENT_0047 evidence simultaneously broadens the allowed reconstruction: Kanade recognizes that she is calmer than before, accepts that trusted people may contribute to that calm, reciprocates Honami's care, wants the four N25 humans to keep making music together, and can value a song for accompanying a listener even below a full `saved` threshold. Area `_006` preserves the pathology by showing that she has eaten only one meal that day.

### Mafuyu - preserve `MF-E0042-01`; add pre-N25 affect/search reconstruction

The flashback establishes that Mafuyu's first strong differentiation inside affective flattening is painful K music rather than a clearly positive preference. She cannot stop listening, seeks a method for understanding why it moves her, and begins reconstructing/composing after her parents sleep. Creative inquiry is therefore self-initiated before Mafuyu has a stable self-definition.

Present evidence remains compatible with `MF-E0042-01`: Mafuyu recognizes that an old `dragon` self-description no longer fits but cannot name the replacement; gives Kanade practical health advice, asks whether the behavior counts as worry, and then continues the care behavior; visits the SEKAI simply to rest; and explicitly judges that meeting Kanade/Ena/Mizuki/Miku and making music with them was, in aggregate, good.

No successor state is warranted because broad ordinary preference access remains poor, linked area `_007` preserves absent favorite-season preference and weak heat/cold discrimination, family/autonomy conflict is unresolved, and categorical self-definition remains incomplete.

### Ena - preserve `E-E0014-01`

Card `0364` reconstructs a middle-school period of paternal discouragement, failure, family-directed anger, and withdrawal. Seeing Airi visibly pursue her idol dream helped Ena decide to try again. Present Ena explicitly interprets herself as someone repeatedly helped by others while preserving the conclusion that her own contribution to N25 should be made through her art. She then uses Mafuyu's early arrangement as creative evidence for improving a current illustration. This strengthens relationally reopened agency and cross-modal creative translation without changing the current Ena state.

### Mizuki - preserve `MZ-E0039-01`

Card `0363` has Mizuki read the Kanade/Mafuyu origin as mutual illumination, then return to Ena's waiting and the time that waiting has preserved. The source leaves an internal comparison unfinished; do not resolve it beyond the text. Mizuki explicitly values the time Ena has given but does not disclose the guarded issue. Area `_005` adds another direct reaffirmation of Ena's waiting. The attachment-preserving disclosure-deferral state therefore remains current.

### Historical-simulation guardrail

For simulation before K/Snow begin collaborating, do not project the present tuple backward. EVENT_0047 supplies a required retrospective supplement: pre-N25 Kanade is more rescue-commanded and physically endangered than current Kanade, while pre-N25 Mafuyu is socially polished but has far less usable first-person affect/preference access than current Mafuyu. These are historical reconstruction notes, not new current-state IDs discovered out of chronological order.

- Wonderlands×Showtime principal humans — `PENDING_MAIN_STORY_FOUNDATION`
- Virtual Singer manifestation-specific states — `PENDING_CONTEXT_MODEL`
- N25 event-era state transitions after EVENT_0058 — `PENDING_EVENT_INTEGRATION` (positive character-state evidence integrated through EVENT_0058)

## 5. Event-integration template

```yaml
state_id: <stable-id>
character: <name>
valid_from: <story/event locator>
valid_through: <story/event locator or null>
status: historical | current_within_analysis_cutoff
previous_state: <id>
transition_class: developmental | refinement | relationship-driven | knowledge-driven
self_concept: ...
primary_goals: ...
active_fears: ...
emotional_baseline: ...
agency_profile: ...
coping_and_defenses: ...
relationship_conditions: ...
knowledge_state_ref: ...
speech_register_changes: ...
transition_trigger: ...
key_evidence: ...
confidence: high | medium | low
next_state: <id | PENDING_EVENT_INTEGRATION>
```

## 6. Guardrails

- Do not create a new state for every event; create one when behaviorally meaningful state has changed.
- Do not overwrite earlier states with later growth.
- Relationship change without broad psychological change belongs primarily in the relationship ledger.
- Knowledge acquisition without psychological change belongs primarily in the epistemic ledger.
- A character can be reconstruction-ready for a historical state even when the latest live-service state is not integrated.


## 4.24 EVENT_0053 character-state integration — Ena outcome-uncertain artistic recommitment

### E-E0053-01 — outcome-uncertain self-authored artistic recommitment

**Character:** Shinonome Ena  
**Valid from:** EVENT_0053 endpoint  
**Previous:** `E-E0014-01`  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.

EVENT_0014 established that uncertainty about talent no longer automatically authorized cessation. EVENT_0053 stress-tests that rule against substantially harsher evidence: Ena voluntarily re-enters the class she previously fled, sees peers who genuinely advanced while she lacked systematic training, receives severe technical criticism she accepts as accurate, and receives no guarantee that continued effort will make her professionally good enough. She chooses continued drawing and training anyway.

**Self-concept:** a flawed, technically incomplete artist whose legitimacy no longer requires a prior guarantee of talent; the repeated `self` assignment can now depict the unglamorous person who struggles at a tablet and continues.

**Primary goals:** acquire fundamentals and expressive range; make N25 images that render what Ena herself receives from Kanade's music and Mafuyu's lyrics; continue developing rather than merely protect current self-esteem.

**Active fears:** being permanently behind; discovering that effort cannot close the gap; public humiliation; wasting time; failing to express what she can internally imagine; credible expert judgment.

**Agency profile:** markedly strengthened under evaluative threat. Ena can initiate feared contact, return to an old failure site, solicit critique, accept actionable negative evaluation, and choose long-horizon practice before confidence arrives.

**Coping / defenses:** comparison, anger, self-attack, overwork, and verbal defensiveness remain active. The new corrective is not serenity but converting criticism and shame into concrete training while refusing to let them settle the question of whether she is permitted to draw.

**Relationship conditions:** N25 support is motivational and evidentiary but not the sole source of artistic permission. Kanade/Mafuyu/Mizuki can validate Ena's two-year creative history without denying her technical deficit.

**Transition trigger:** inability to express the new N25 song -> return to Yukihira's class -> accurate peer/teacher comparison -> paternal challenge about enduring artistic pain -> N25 archive-based correction of the `two blank years` narrative -> completed repeated self-assignment -> explicit continuation despite uncertain possibility.

**Key reconstruction rule:** criticism can still hurt intensely, but accurate criticism is increasingly usable as training data rather than an automatic domain-exit command.

**Negative constraints:** do not model Ena as cured of recognition hunger, jealousy, comparison, or defensiveness; do not infer professional success; do not make Yukihira's approval the causal solution; do not confuse recommitment with healthy pacing.

**Next:** `PENDING_EVENT_INTEGRATION`.


## 4.25 EVENT_0058 character-state integration — chosen school participation without successor state

**Event:** `EVENT_0058 — 白熱！神高応援団！`.  
**Impact:** Tier B relationship / secondary Tier C characterization and Tier D ordinary-life; **I2** in mature N25 scope.  
**Bounded authority:** `PJSK_EVENT_0058_DEEP_READING.md`.

### Mizuki — preserve `MZ-E0039-01`

EVENT_0058 strongly cross-validates that Mizuki's school avoidance is **context-conditioned rather than a generalized absence of social appetite**. Mizuki begins from explicit aversion to a conspicuous cheer-squad role and partly instrumental attendance logic, but sustained contact with a sincere recipient-centered goal, a creative role with real agency, An's safe friendship, and Rui's trusted collaboration converts obligation into chosen effort and genuine enjoyment.

Card `0429` materially expands reconstruction competence: Mizuki researches unfamiliar historical material, identifies dramatic function before visual execution, storyboards a cold-colored flame that must nevertheless read as warm encouragement, iterates materials, and requests a bounded music suggestion from Kanade while explicitly preserving Kanade's right not to help. The endpoint is first-person rather than observer-imposed: Mizuki admits that what initially felt like a terrible draw became genuinely fun and later attends the cheer-group barbecue.

**Reconstruction refinement:** `identity-threat-conditioned school discomfort + pragmatic attendance` can become `high-effort collective participation + genuine enjoyment` when role fit, relational safety, and self-authored purpose are sufficient. This does **not** imply global school enthusiasm, assimilation, or increased readiness to disclose the guarded issue.

### Other N25 humans

Preserve `MF-E0042-01 / K-E0026-01 / E-E0053-01`. Kanade supplies a bounded off-circle creative assist; Ena supplies relationship/observer evidence around Mizuki; neither warrants a successor global state.

### Authority result

Current N25 human-state tuple remains:

`MF-E0042-01 / K-E0026-01 / E-E0053-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.

## 4.26 EVENT_0061 state integration — embodied autonomy divergence / chosen relational refuge

### `MF-E0061-01 — embodied autonomy divergence / chosen relational refuge`

**Boundary:** end of complete `EVENT_0061 — 迷い子の手を引く、そのさきは` envelope.  
**Previous:** `MF-E0042-01`.  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.  
**Transition class:** Tier A developmental / I3.

EVENT_0061 makes the relational warmth established by EVENT_0042 behaviorally consequential under direct conflict with externally authored obligation. Mafuyu still describes her mother's future-oriented reasoning as correct and still values genuine maternal care, but the mock-exam route produces body-level refusal signals—heaviness, coldness, nausea, breathlessness—and she goes to Phoenix Wonderland instead of taking the exam before she can explain why. Later, knowing the night show will violate curfew, she chooses to stay.

At the park, being found by Kanade/Ena/Mizuki and holding Kanade's hand lets Mafuyu identify a context-level first-person fact: `“ここ”は……あったかいんだ`. Card `0445:02` then shows deliberate refuge-seeking: unable to sleep at home, Mafuyu enters Empty SEKAI and asks Miku and Len to remain beside her because `ここは、冷たくない`.

**State statement:** `relational/contribution-linked warmth + preserved self-search + body-level detection of expectation conflict + behavior that can diverge from externally authored correctness before verbal explanation + explicit context-level recognition of N25/SEKAI warmth + deliberate refuge-seeking/co-presence + continued creative self-line under family restriction + unresolved maternal attachment and future-authority conflict`.

**Reconstruction rules:** Mafuyu may act autonomously before she can formulate an autonomous principle; may experience conflict somatically while continuing to call the imposed path correct; may feel guilt/confusion after divergence rather than confident rebellion; may intentionally seek N25/SEKAI co-presence as low-demand relief; and may tactically preserve lyric/music work under household restriction.

**Guardrail:** do not reconstruct this as generalized rebellion, rejection of her mother, broad preference recovery, a settled music-career choice, fluent emotion naming, friendship cure, or N25-as-replacement-family. Genuine maternal care and autonomy-eroding control remain co-present.

**Key evidence:** core `0061:02-08`; Mafuyu card `0445:01-02`; Kanade card `0449:01-02`; Len card `0447:01-02`; linked `areatalk_ev_night_09_001-005` and `monthly2207_004-005`.

### Other N25 humans

Preserve `K-E0026-01 / E-E0053-01 / MZ-E0039-01`. Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki receive relationship/reconstruction refinements but no successor global state.

### Authority result

Current N25 human-state tuple becomes:

`MF-E0061-01 / K-E0026-01 / E-E0053-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.


## EVENT_0063 state integration — Kanade developmental transition

### K-E0063-01 — reciprocal self-permission / bounded non-instrumental living

**Boundary:** end of EVENT_0063 `みんなでエンジョイ！スポジョイパーク`.  
**Previous state:** `K-E0026-01`.  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.  
**Transition class:** developmental / self-directed positive affect and reciprocal permission without rescue-resolution.

- Kanade explicitly experiences ordinary recreation as `楽しい`, then notices the guilt that frames nonproductive pleasure as time stolen from saving others.
- Minori and Luka provide a reciprocal-care bridge: people Kanade cares about may also want Kanade herself to smile. This makes bounded self-directed enjoyment morally legible without requiring Kanade to possess a fully intrinsic self-worth model.
- Kanade chooses strike-out because she remembers enjoying it with her father (`すごく楽しかったから、またやってみたい`) and decides to enjoy the activity with the group.
- She agrees that she would like to gather again occasionally for non-composition activity and tells Luka she will try to value her own feelings a little more.
- Rescue/penance remains active. Kanade still frames exercise primarily as maintenance for composing/saving, risks overdoing health behavior once it becomes mission-linked, and retains poor body regulation.
- Ordinary social infrastructure expands: contact exchange, group chat, casual stickers, mutual music-shop invitation with Ichika, and willingness to continue cross-unit friendship.
- Positive father memory broadens again from musical/aesthetic inheritance into ordinary play/procedural teaching.
- **Guardrail:** do not infer rescue-mission resolution, healthy self-care, broad intrinsic self-worth, extroversion, or freedom from guilt about nonproductive time.

- **Confidence:** high.

- Ena: preserve `E-E0053-01`; EVENT_0063 adds practical-research, beauty/makeup expertise, reciprocal ordinary-care, and cross-unit social evidence.

## EVENT_0068 state integration — chosen approach under fear / causal backfill

**Event:** `EVENT_0068 — そしていま、リボンを結んで`.

EVENT_0068 materially revises the causal reading of Mizuki's current disclosure state without replacing it. The flashback establishes that joining K/Ena was already a self-authored approach toward a wanted creative/relational place **while rejection fear remained active**. Mizuki verifies K's contact, negotiates a first creative disagreement with Ena, wants the invitation to continue, then hesitates because people one wants to be with are precisely the people whose rejection can hurt most. The older sister's intervention does not remove fear; it distinguishes fear-plus-deliberation from the earlier surrender state and authorizes Mizuki to approach a place they want.

Card `0486` supplies present first-person validation: reviewing accumulated N25 photos, Mizuki recognizes that they are genuinely smiling/enjoying the life produced by that earlier approach and wants to thank the sister for helping make it possible.

**Current-state effect:** strengthen `MZ-E0039-01` by clarifying that attachment-preserving disclosure deferral occurs *after* an earlier successful act of fearful approach. Nondisclosure is therefore not evidence that Mizuki has never been capable of courage, approach, or authentic belonging.

**Guardrail:** no guarded-content disclosure occurs; fear of relationship change remains; do not create `MZ-E0068-01` or treat retrospective happiness as disclosure readiness.

### Mafuyu — preserve `MF-E0061-01`

Card `0488` extends family-concealment evidence: Mafuyu anticipates maternal discovery and hides lyric material, wonders whether concealment will continue indefinitely, and receives a sibling-care comparison from Shizuku. This strengthens the mixed-valence care/autonomy model but does not create open household rebellion or a new global state.

### Kanade — preserve `K-E0063-01`

Card `0487` adds a bounded ordinary experiment: café co-working proves poorly suited to Kanade's concentration while still exposing her to the others' work processes and possibly contributing later creative input. Kanade can retain information from a mixed/failed low-risk ordinary experiment without needing to declare the outing useless.

### Ena — preserve `E-E0053-01`

The founding flashback clarifies why K/N25 creative recognition has unusual force for Ena: after blocked art and easier appearance-based feedback, K's song reactivates drawing and the first MV makes Ena's image specifically useful to a shared work. This is causal backfill, not a new present artistic state.

`EVENT_0067` and `EVENT_0069` are complete-envelope N25 I0 screens. Current N25 human-state tuple therefore remains:

`MF-E0061-01 / K-E0063-01 / E-E0053-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.

## EVENT_0070 state integration — Ena self-authored aesthetic valuation

### `E-E0070-01 — self-authored aesthetic valuation / technique-expression integration`

**Boundary:** end of complete `EVENT_0070 — 好きを描いて♪レインボーキャンバス` envelope.  
**Previous:** `E-E0053-01`.  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.  
**Transition class:** Tier A developmental / I3.

EVENT_0053 established outcome-uncertain self-authored artistic recommitment: Ena could acknowledge real technical deficit and uncertain future talent while still choosing to continue. EVENT_0070 changes the evaluation architecture inside that recommitment. Through Honami's drawing problem, the child's rejection of a technically corrected crocodile, Futaba's imitation history, and Ena's own post-critique reflection, Ena distinguishes **recognizability/finish** from **person-specific expressive value**.

The decisive first-person evidence is Ena's ability to look at a technically flawed work and say `私は好きだな、この絵`, followed by the goal `私が納得できる絵が描けるように——頑張っていこう！`. Card `0499` confirms persistence: she continues teaching close observation and fundamentals while also recognizing that drawing with Honami/Emu restores a `純粋に、絵を楽しもう` orientation.

**State statement:** `outcome-uncertain self-authored recommitment + accurate awareness of technical deficits + continued respect for professional/fundamental training + explicit separation of technical recognizability from person-specific expressive value + first-person aesthetic liking as legitimate evidence + goal-sensitive expression-preserving pedagogy + renewed non-instrumental enjoyment of drawing + continuing criticism/comparison sensitivity and technical ambition`.

**Guardrail:** do not reconstruct this as anti-technique, anti-criticism, stable self-esteem, professional confidence, or “anything Ena likes is automatically good.” External technical judgment and first-person aesthetic judgment are now co-authorities rather than mutually exclusive replacements.

**Key evidence:** core `0070:01-08`; cards `0499:01-02`, `0500:01-02`, `0502:01-02`, `0503:01-02`; linked `areatalk_ev_shuffle_23_001-005`.

### Other N25 humans after EVENT_0070

Preserve `MF-E0061-01 / K-E0063-01 / MZ-E0039-01`. Kanade receives reliance/relationship evidence through the Honami referral; no successor state is warranted.

**Current tuple after EVENT_0070:** `MF-E0061-01 / K-E0063-01 / E-E0070-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.

## EVENT_0072 state integration — Mafuyu articulated positive wanting

### `MF-E0072-01 — articulated positive wanting / self-authored participation`

**Boundary:** end of complete `EVENT_0072 — この祭に 夕闇色も` envelope.  
**Previous:** `MF-E0061-01`.  
**Status:** **current within N25 analysis cutoff**.  
**Transition class:** Tier A developmental / I3.

EVENT_0061 established behaviorally consequential autonomy before a fluent autonomous principle. EVENT_0072 supplies the next step. Mafuyu increasingly protects self-chosen action through institutionally legible rationale, notices repeated salience when other people act toward goals they can call their own, receives positive self-evidence from competent practical care and gratitude, and finally converts salience into direct action-specific desire language.

At the street piano Kanade asks `ピアノ、弾きたい？`. Mafuyu answers `……やりたい……`, then `……うん` / `——少し、弾いてみたい`. Asked later whether she is having fun, she still cannot categorize the emotion but can state a continuation preference: `もう少しだけ、こうしていたい`.

**State statement:** `EVENT_0061 embodied autonomy divergence + continuing maternal/future-authority conflict + increasingly strategic protection of self-chosen action + recurrent salience around other people's chosen goals + positive self-evidence from useful caregiving and gratitude + first explicit action-specific positive-desire language + ability to enact that desire and prefer continuation without categorical emotion naming + music restored as a personally wantable activity + broad life goal/career preference unresolved`.

**Reconstruction rules:** Mafuyu may now name a specific immediate want when salience is strong enough; may still answer `わからない` to broader affect or identity questions; may use socially acceptable secondary rationale to protect a private initiating motive; and may derive positive evidence from helping without yet knowing whether medicine is a chosen future.

**Guardrail:** nursing/medical-school salience, first-aid competence, relief after gratitude, and recovery follow-up do **not** establish a doctor/nurse vocation. Likewise, wanting to play piano does not establish a music career. Genuine maternal care and autonomy-eroding control remain co-present.

**Key evidence:** core `0072:01-10`, especially `0072:02`, `0072:04`, `0072:07-08`, and `0072:10`; Mafuyu card `0511:01-02`; bounded Shizuku/Rui/Akito/Honami card continuities.

### Other N25 humans after EVENT_0072

Preserve `K-E0063-01 / E-E0070-01 / MZ-E0039-01`. Kanade's first-person-authority prompting is relationship evidence rather than a successor Kanade state.

**Current tuple:** `MF-E0072-01 / K-E0063-01 / E-E0070-01 / MZ-E0039-01`.

## EVENT_0076 characterization increment — recipient-led teaching and reciprocal reception

**Impact:** I1, Tier C characterization / Tier B relationship / Tier D ordinary creative and domestic life. Preserve `K-E0063-01`; no successor human state.

Kanade asks what Ichika wants the song to communicate, hears a trace of that intention before its explanation, and teaches a concrete instrument-balance method rather than replacing the learner's work. She recognizes standing beside important people as a possible way of saving, receives creative inspiration and becomes absorbed in her own phrase before apologizing for the attention shift (`PJSK:event:0076:03:001:0031–0090`; `PJSK:event:0076:03:002:0005–0019`; `PJSK:event:0076:04:002:0011–0024`). She teaches through calm questions, precise procedures, qualified judgment and acknowledgment of her own early difficulty (`PJSK:event:0076:04:002:0025–0038`). Honami and Ichika independently apply the idea to their lives; Kanade does not claim expertise over those histories. The ordinary rice-ball hesitation preserves context dependence rather than universal decisiveness.

Kanade later credits Ichika's persistence, thanks her for inspiration, enjoys the selected cream puffs, recognizes her flower-selection contribution and intends to care for the gerberas so she can enjoy them longer (`PJSK:card:0535:02:001:0005–0036`, `PJSK:card:0535:02:001:0039–0060`). Honami's report of an often empty refrigerator, cup-noodle reliance and prepared food disappearing between visits preserves dependence on household support; it does not certify recovered self-regulation (`PJSK:card:0536:01:001:0022–0037`).

Mafuyu has only an indirect prior-conversation reference in card 0536:01; Ena and Mizuki have no new direct evidence. Preserve the complete tuple `MF-E0072-01 / K-E0063-01 / E-E0070-01 / MZ-E0039-01`. ## EVENT_0077 character refinement — differentiated agency support and collaborative wanting

**Event impact I3 through relationship/epistemic change; character-level interpretation refined without a successor global human ID.** Preserve `MF-E0072-01 / K-E0063-01 / E-E0070-01 / MZ-E0039-01`. The [complete 23-surface reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/N25/PJSK_EVENT_0077_DEEP_READING.md) owns shared causes and the exact witness register.

- **Ena / `E-E0070-01`: material refinement.** Her hatching assignment lacks an owned intention despite a plausible subject. Learning that Mafuyu already tried to state her wish corrects Ena's assumption that simple assertiveness is the missing step. She considers the difference between their parental environments, asks what Mafuyu wants and provides a bounded place to do it (`PJSK:event:0077:06:002:0020–0084`). The phone intervention includes taking over and fabricated justification; effective care is not proof of consistently consent-sensitive practice. During collaborative work she recognizes Mafuyu's authored intention, draws the working face, and explicitly qualifies the portrait as her own potentially mistaken seeing (`PJSK:event:0077:07:002:0120–0173`). Her wish for Mafuyu's future freedom supplies sincere expressive content that the teacher can read despite continued technical defects and an exceeded time limit (`PJSK:event:0077:08:004:0002–0019`). This applies and tests the EVENT_0070 co-authority model, not a new stable confidence or anti-technique state.
- **Mafuyu / `MF-E0072-01`: material refinement.** She can say she wants to make songs with the group while remaining uncertain about what music means, why she acts against her mother's wishes, or her own happiness (`PJSK:event:0077:06:002:0040–0071`; `PJSK:event:0077:07:002:0125–0129`). Disclosure of an earlier expressed wish prevents treating EVENT_0072's wording as a first-ever linguistic capacity. At the Shinonome dinner she feels lighter without understanding why similar parental words feel different; public food approval does not certify taste recovery. Afterwards she values an unplanned hour of talk with Mizuki despite pending obligations (`PJSK:card:0542:02:002:0053–0071`). Earlier shallow sleep/mistakes and the sleepless return to cram school preserve significant strain; no clinical diagnosis or global recovery follows.
- **Kanade / `K-E0063-01`: I1 ordinary reciprocity.** She identifies concrete benefits from Honami's careful paid work, asks Ena/Mizuki for gift advice, chooses a familiar favourite when novelty fails, buys it and invites shared enjoyment (`PJSK:card:0541:01:001:0031–0066`; `PJSK:card:0541:02:003:0002–0017`). Fatigue, clutter and dependence on help remain in the area layer. Proposed walking and a pillow trial are not completed health improvements.
- **Mizuki / `MZ-E0039-01`: bounded continuation.** Pacing and joking support Mafuyu's ordinary conversation; school reluctance, work/attendance motives, household food preference and peer banter coexist. The stated pillow reason for declining a sleepover is not disproven, and no guarded-content disclosure is established (`PJSK:card:0543:01:002:0056–0079`; `PJSK:card:0543:02`).

Ena's later thought that her father had never forbidden drawing briefly differentiates his injury from Mafuyu's constraint; she promptly dismisses the thought as pity and continues avoidance (`PJSK:card:0540:02:001:0042–0056`). No reconciliation. Area `night_11_005` preserves envy and irritation at Mafuyu's dexterity alongside plural artistic value. N25 Len's learned usefulness of presence is manifestation-specific, recorded in the relationship ledger; it is not a new human state.

**Prospective default:** retain the human tuple, apply `REL-N25-EM-5` and the EVENT_0077 knowledge/claim refinements. Latest human-state transition remains EVENT_0072; latest integrated evidence EVENT_0077. Next forward candidate EVENT_0078.

## EVENT_0079 characterization increment — sibling observation and bounded restraint

**N25 I1; preserve Ena `E-E0070-01` and all other human defaults.** Card `PJSK:card:0551:01:002:0002–0048` adds a relationship-conditioned ordinary mode without changing Ena's global artistic model. Seeing her sweaty brother, she combines complaint with practical accommodation: she gives him her intended bath slot and asks for gratitude. On closer inspection she infers crying from redness beside his eyes, explains his familiar rubbing habit, and reports having left him alone after a much worse distressed return near the beginning of his musical activity. Akito had not recognized this visible habit himself.

The inference concerns a sign, not its full cause. Ena receives no account of the present Taiga conversation, old Crawl Green failure or Ken's intervention. Her earlier decision to leave space is a newly reported historical episode, not a new present acquisition of empathy, a claim of always respecting privacy, or retrospective knowledge of musical events. Akito's current denial and internal embarrassment do not negate the practical care.

Indirect ordinary evidence: Akito reports Ena talking about seasonal sweets promotions during meals (`PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_street_11_003:01:001:0002–0006`) and a household tradition of direct gift requests, adding that Ena would otherwise complain (`PJSK:card:0555:01:002:0023–0036`). Preserve attribution rather than inventing a depicted present demand. The core's remembered permission to try music before deciding on seriousness (`PJSK:event:0079:04:003:0032–0036`) reiterates EVENT_0029 and is not double-counted as a new causal discovery.

Reconstruction uses the existing chain from person-specific observation through practical response to abrasive, sibling-conditioned speech. No successor human state, new claim or theme/motif ID is warranted. VBS's major Akito/Toya/An/Kohane material remains deferred for its missing foundation; it is not imported as mature N25 character authority. See the [shared EVENT_0079 reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/VBS/PJSK_EVENT_0079_DEEP_READING.md). Next forward event EVENT_0080.

## EVENT_0080 characterization refinement — skill, regulation and received support

**I3 through bounded relationship/epistemic change; preserve Mafuyu `MF-E0072-01`.** The new [mixed reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/MIXED/PJSK_EVENT_0080_DEEP_READING.md) supplies pre-high-school/first-year history as newly revealed evidence, not a new current acquisition of all depicted traits. Historical public enjoyment statements must not become proof of original private motivation.

Mafuyu learns archery rapidly but still uses a demonstrated correction after initial difficulty, explains the movement to peers and later practises privately. She tells Shizuku she comes because she is not consistently composed: concentrating on one shot lets her quiet other concerns. Her instruction helps Shizuku hit for the first time (`PJSK:event:0080:02:008:0072–0083`; `PJSK:event:0080:03:004:0035–0088`). A subsequent frighteningly intense but accurate snowy practice establishes the limit of reading internal condition from results. Her inaudible muttering remains incomplete; Shizuku's interpretation is not omniscient (`PJSK:event:0080:04:005:0024–0074`).

Present maternal concern connects club participation to cold-weather/study risk; Mafuyu postpones immediate withdrawal by citing younger members but does not assert an independent long-term club choice. A study explanation helps delay going home. An ordinary maternal return message unsettles her enough to alter her technique; Shizuku's returned breathing cue helps the next shot (`PJSK:event:0080:05:001:0006–0017`; `PJSK:event:0080:05:003:0008–0017`; `PJSK:event:0080:07:001:0030–0079`). Skill, bodily strain, selective explanation and underarticulated avoidance coexist. Neither club resignation nor restored general pleasure/taste/vocational certainty occurs.

After hearing that Shizuku wants to support her, Mafuyu initially retains exam/fourth-place explanations. Later, a reminder of retirement leaves her cold; seeing the snow rabbits retrieves the explicit support promise, and she tentatively notices feeling less cold before doubting it (`PJSK:card:0557:01:004:0002–0029`). This recipient-side sequence is real analytical evidence without a confident emotional label, complete causal appraisal or durable symptom cure. Preserve the distinction from Shizuku's uncertain assessment of whether she helped.

Ordinary capacities include a tied match and rematch proposal; checking Shizuku after a fall, sharing an umbrella, laughing at snow on her face and wiping it away; identifying a wrong branch map; asking to accompany an actual umbrella purchase; accepting a lunch invitation after an existing commitment is checked; and explaining topical-fiction reading as study (`PJSK:event:0080:08:001:0002–0052`; `PJSK:card:0557:02:002:0017–0051`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_shuffle_26_001:01`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_shuffle_26_002:01`). These acts are not dismissed as fake, but public enjoyment wording alone does not establish affective fluency. In the holiday scene, private concern that maternal presence constrains N25 work accompanies the public spare-time explanation; no single motive exhausts the outing.

Reconstruction must apply the new cross-unit relationship and knowledge state while retaining the human tuple and EVENT_0077's limit on first-ever desire language. Kanade, Ena and Mizuki receive no new global characterization from this envelope. Shizuku's perfection pressure, agency, hobbies and care, Airi's independent tea interests, and Shiho's family/play evidence remain routed for founded MMJ/Leo/need comparison. Next EVENT_0081 after transaction closure.

## EVENT_0081 characterization increment — public help and ordinary reception

**I1; preserve all four human defaults.** In the [complete mixed reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/MIXED/PJSK_EVENT_0081_DEEP_READING.md), Kanade notices that interrupted music is losing a show's audience, uses an available piano and develops the fragment she heard according to the scene's needs. Her rapid action includes represented musical reasoning; it is not full-score recall or an unqualified compulsion to intervene in everything (`PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0071–0084`). Tsukasa identifies how the accompaniment helped his acting. Kanade initially minimizes her work, then thanks the friends who praise it (`PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0091–0111`). This strengthens established creative support beyond Mafuyu, not a new global public-confidence state.

Miku's comparison between the play's smile-making and Kanade's work prompts Kanade to remember her happiness at Mafuyu's smile (`PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0037–0050`). Partial positive consequence is already meaningful under `K-E0063-01`; persistent rescue/guilt architecture remains. Shopping for a small decoration to bring to a hospitalized family member extends remembered household ritual into intended care. She is physically tired carrying purchases and considers Honami's later plans; no hospital visit or response is shown (`PJSK:event:0081:07:005:0002–0008`).

Mafuyu draws a fortune for Len, reassures him about her own poor result, recognizes the earlier festival piano by its moon mark, accommodates Miku's discreet watching and offers both public and plainer peer praise (`PJSK:event:0081:06:002:0011–0017`; `PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0005–0008`; `PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0017–0035`; `PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0096–0109`). She accepts Shizuku's sweets invitation until family contact, preserving its condition rather than displaying unlimited autonomy or restored taste (`PJSK:event:0081:07:004:0005–0018`). Preserve `REL-CROSS-MAFUYU-SHIZUKU-E0080` and its knowledge limits.

Ena candidly enjoys the show and Kanade's playing, while her later running follows an Akito remark she privately withholds behind a show-inspired explanation (`PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0107–0111`; `PJSK:event:0081:07:005:0009–0021`). Mizuki openly pursues a favorite accessory sale, includes others, enjoys ritual, teases and enforces the singers' concealment boundary (`PJSK:event:0081:06:002:0010–0014`; `PJSK:event:0081:06:003:0011–0031`). None discloses Mizuki's guarded content or replaces their existing state.

Empty Len reports settling through others' kindness and learning string figures from Luka, then requests Rin's attention to a practiced ladder. The attempt is proposed, not shown completed (`PJSK:area:areatalk_monthly2212_005:01`). Keep this manifestation's ordinary evidence separate from the other four units' singers. Next EVENT_0082 after closure.

## EVENT_0083 characterization increment — attentive feedback and ordinary difficulty

**I1; preserve all four human defaults.** Kanade directly tells Ichika that she has watched both the software-Miku and band videos, praises both performance and song, and predicts further growth. This adds a concrete instance of attention to an established learner/friend's work after the EVENT_0076 lesson; it does not establish that Kanade caused all view growth or that the predicted future audience has already arrived (`PJSK:card:0580:01:001:0035–0047`).

Kanade's fragmented messages disclose difficulty entering text on a phone and an accidental premature send. She later reports getting somewhat used to input. Ichika offers time, and Kanade answers with a thanks sticker; the exchange ends with matching goodnight stickers. Musical competence coexists with a small ordinary skill difficulty and reciprocal accommodation. This is neither general technological incompetence nor a new global sociability/confidence state. Ichika's prior assumption that Kanade is currently composing is unconfirmed (`PJSK:card:0580:01:001:0025–0049`).

Ichika recalls Honami's request to help choose Kanade's next flowers; the following scene shows flowers selected for an acquaintance's home. Kanade's receipt, reaction and knowledge of the planning remain unshown (`PJSK:card:0580:01:001:0052–0053`; `PJSK:card:0580:02:002:0018–0034`). Preserve `K-E0063-01` with its established ordinary-life possibilities and unresolved rescue/guilt architecture, and preserve all Mafuyu/Ena/Mizuki defaults. The [complete reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/LEO_NEED/PJSK_EVENT_0083_DEEP_READING.md) retains substantial Honami/Leo/need and other-unit evidence for later founded interpretation. Next EVENT_0084 after closure.

## EVENT_0084 characterization refinement — supported skill and shared pleasure

**I2 for Kanade; preserve `K-E0063-01` and the other human defaults.** Kanade can want to care for others, depend on help with one task and learn another task well enough to guide someone else. She offers her home, tries to serve Honami on her non-work time, accepts help with a heavy kettle, follows practical instruction, completes candles and later performs the physical making for phone-connected Len. His purposes and choices guide the second activity; her competence is supported and domain-specific, neither global helplessness nor universal independence (`PJSK:event:0084:03:003:0006–0014`; `PJSK:event:0084:04:001`; `PJSK:event:0084:05:001:0045–0049`; `PJSK:event:0084:06:003:0002–0062`).

She seeks Len after remembering a subdued fragment, offers listening without assuming its cause, and gives him a choice about collaborating. When he credits her, she credits his initiative; his pleasure in making together helps her identify her own. She later thanks him explicitly. Recognizing the newly quiet house, using Ichika's gift and voluntarily seeking ordinary company extend EVENT_0042/0063's established ordinary-life permission, not a first discovery of joy or a cure of rescue obligation (`PJSK:event:0084:06:001`; `PJSK:event:0084:06:002`; `PJSK:event:0084:06:003:0064–0087`; `PJSK:event:0084:07:003:0060–0074`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_shuffle_28_003:01`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_ev_shuffle_28_004:01`). Her care-sensitive wait for Honami's meal and fatigue before the CD errand preserve uneven self-care and stamina (`PJSK:event:0084:02:003:0002–0034`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_monthly2301_004:01`).

**Other N25 evidence is I1.** Mafuyu seeks classmates' recommendation and queues for a bought gift; her gratitude is not inferior to handmade care. She later reports a small concentration/clearing response to Kanade's candle and a study-compatible explanation that seemed acceptable to her mother. Neither general sensory/affective recovery nor broader household permission follows (`PJSK:event:0084:07:003:0010–0016`; `PJSK:card:0585:02:001:0033–0053`). Ena combines attractive/selfie-conscious presentation with named recipient care, initially preserves Kanade's gift as precious and then actually uses it; Mizuki uses hers during work breaks and enjoys craft talk beyond utility (`PJSK:event:0084:07:003:0020–0028`; `PJSK:card:0585:02:001:0021–0063`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_monthly2301_003:01`). Their governing states and Mizuki's guarded content remain unchanged.

Empty Len already wants to give and seeks several singers' help before Kanade intervenes. Miku's unresolved discussion still helps him tentatively recognize pleasure in thinking of others; later he contributes choices, receives credit and finally smells the result. Practical confidence remains uneven, as shown by accepting MEIKO’s offered help with lighting it and her prompting him to verbalize a later play request (`PJSK:card:0587:01:001`; `PJSK:card:0587:02:001`; `PJSK:area:areatalk_monthly2301_002:01`). This manifestation remains distinct from the three other SEKAI systems in the [complete reading](../03_SEQUENTIAL_EVENT_READINGS/MIXED/PJSK_EVENT_0084_DEEP_READING.md). Next EVENT_0085 after closure.

Rin says she has already read it, then agrees while describing herself as bored; Len accepts positively. The loan is reported completed, the invitation/acceptance is directly shown, and a finished shared reading is not. No book title, therapeutic purpose, Ena's private motive or first-ever companionship is established (`PJSK:area:areatalk_monthly2302_001:01:001:0002–0009`).
